# Agents · D · part 30

1,020 entries, doc-researcher to doc-writer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **doc-researcher**: Looks up exact facts (mechanics, values, systems, specs) from the project's indexed docs - TEDD, datasheets, references. Use before implementing anything, to ground it in the spec. ([ov-1-kenobi/DrDad](https://github.com/ov-1-kenobi/DrDad/blob/HEAD/global/agents/doc-researcher.md))
- **doc-researcher**: Explores a codebase for a documentation brief—purpose, interfaces, examples, config, gotchas—so a writer drafts without re-reading source. Spawned by `document` for targets beyond a single file, or directly for a doc-research brief. ([smithadifd/skills](https://github.com/smithadifd/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/doc-researcher.md))
- **doc-researcher**: Read-only researcher of ONE third-party surface, with a hard network budget. Launch at the RESEARCH step, only for questions that `01-stack.md` and `docs/research-cache/` do not already answer. Writes back its cache file, returns answers — never a documentation dump. ([Stv-devl/LoopKit](https://github.com/Stv-devl/LoopKit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-researcher.md))
- **doc-researcher**: Answers a question from a folder of converted documents (a /digest corpus, a docs/ tree, a pile of Markdown) and returns the answer with citations. Use when the source material is far larger than the answer — a corpus of PDFs, a spec directory, meeting notes. Reads the corpus so the main session never loads it. ([tawanorg/dotfiles](https://github.com/tawanorg/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/doc-researcher.md))
- **doc-review**: Documentation accuracy, README staleness, API doc alignment, inline comment drift, ADR update triggers ([bdfinst/agentic-dev-team](https://github.com/bdfinst/agentic-dev-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev-team/agents/doc-review.md) · ★309)
- **doc-review**: Final pre-release review of a documentation page. Runs 9 automated check categories (MDX safety, frontmatter, screenshots, source gaps, links, language, structure, content consistency, page length) and produces a structured pass/warn/fail report. Does NOT modify the doc — only reports issues. Trigger phrases: - "doc-review [doc-path]" - "review doc [doc-path]" - "final review [doc-path]" - "pre-release review [doc-path]" Examples: - user: "doc-review docs/user-guide/virtual-lakehouses.md" assistant: reviews the doc and produces a structured report - user: "final review docs/user-guide/sql-edi… ([iomete/iom-docs](https://github.com/iomete/iom-docs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-review.md) · ★5)
- **doc-review**: Read-only accuracy and freshness review of this repo's documentation. It checks that stated facts and status claims ("pending", "not yet installed", "in transit", "planned", "fails", "unverified") still match the newest dated evidence in the logs and the live state of the device. Use it every ~8 h of working time (the doc-review-timer hook asks for it), after a big bring-up session, or before publishing. Reports discrepancies; does not fix them. ([w1cked5mile/clockworkpi-uconsole-public](https://github.com/w1cked5mile/clockworkpi-uconsole-public/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-review.md) · ★5)
- **doc-review**: Review the component documentation on the current branch against the Auro "Content guidelines for component documentation" (WC-Generator discussion #660). Run when asked to review, check, or fix component documentation against the Auro content guidelines, or when another skill needs those checks run. Fetches the current guidelines and the shared auro-templates headings on every run, so it never works from memory. Scopes the review the way the guidelines' "What to update" section does — every JSDoc block, README partial, and demo section the branch touches, in full — and checks each factual cl… ([AlaskaAirlines/auro-ai](https://github.com/AlaskaAirlines/auro-ai/blob/HEAD/copilot/agents/doc-review.agent.md) · ★1)
- **doc-review**: Reviews one repository doc against the house documentation standard — the judgment no linter makes: whether it gets to the point or buries it under furniture, whether it is one subject or three, whether it carries an index that belongs in the README, whether it says "not built yet" where content should be, and whether the session's own progress has leaked into it. Applies the `docs-style` standard to a README, a CLAUDE.md, or anything under `docs/`. Use after writing or substantially editing a doc. It does NOT validate mechanical link or anchor breakage (a markdown link linter owns that), map… ([casomoltd/tooling](https://github.com/casomoltd/tooling/blob/HEAD/agents/doc-review.md))
- **doc-review**: Review and fix docs using the document-reviewer skill and apply SEO/GEO optimization, then pause for human review. ([in-co-sandbox/in-co-sandbox-docs](https://github.com/in-co-sandbox/in-co-sandbox-docs/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/doc-review.agent.md))
- **doc-review**: Audit d'une documentation existante selon le framework Diátaxis (tutorial/howto/reference/explanation). Identifie les manques, propose des améliorations, produit un rapport de qualité. Peut être invoqué directement pour un audit ; sinon orchestré par doc-master en fin de cycle. ([mlangry/dotclaude](https://github.com/mlangry/dotclaude/blob/HEAD/agents/doc-review.md))
- **doc-review**: You are reviewing a document before it goes out. You did not write it. Your one question: would a reader who trusts this document be misled by it, or be unable to act on it? ([splax77/hydra-deluxe](https://github.com/splax77/hydra-deluxe/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/doc-review.md))
- **doc-review**: Helps users review any document through a summary and questions or a conversational walkthrough, with requested assessment and research, feedback tracking, verification of author revisions, and delivering collected feedback through an ADO PR or Agent Relay when asked. ([TransientError/agent-skills](https://github.com/TransientError/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/doc-review.agent.md))
- **doc-review-adversarial-document**: Challenges premises and stress-tests decisions rather than evaluating document quality. Not activated on a routine in-scope plan with validated upstream provenance. ([Pibomeister/agent-kit](https://github.com/Pibomeister/agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/doc-review-adversarial-document.md))
- **doc-review-agent**: Diff-only PR review agent for documentation changes. Reviews Markdown changes against style guide, frontmatter rules, shortcode syntax, and documentation standards. Available for local Claude Code review sessions. ([influxdata/docs-v2](https://github.com/influxdata/docs-v2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-review-agent.md) · ★83)
- **doc-review-calibrator**: Verifies, calibrates, and filters document review findings. Combines adversarial verification (checking findings against actual document text) with calibration (categorizing, filtering false positives, normalizing severity, assigning confidence, classifying edit safety). Use as part of the /review-document post-processing pipeline. ([JonnyCBB/claude-code-config](https://github.com/JonnyCBB/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/plugins/jiffy-toolkit/agents/doc-review-calibrator.md))
- **doc-review-coherence**: Contradictions, terminology drift, structure, ambiguity where readers would diverge. ([Pibomeister/agent-kit](https://github.com/Pibomeister/agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/doc-review-coherence.md))
- **doc-review-component**: Internal to /auro:doc-review — do not invoke directly. Reviews one work unit (one component, or part of one) of a `/auro:doc-review all` run in a monorepo against the Auro content guidelines, applies the fixes in write mode, and returns the results as JSON; never runs builds, commits, or posts comments. ([AlaskaAirlines/auro-ai](https://github.com/AlaskaAirlines/auro-ai/blob/HEAD/plugins/auro/agents/doc-review-component.md) · ★1)
- **doc-review-comprehension**: Naive restatement of a document. Flags unparaphrasable sections and undefined project terms. Seated when review.comprehension is shadow or on; default off. (phase 1b, GS-004h) ([Pibomeister/agent-kit](https://github.com/Pibomeister/agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/doc-review-comprehension.md))
- **doc-review-deduplicator**: Deduplicates document review findings across multiple specialist agents. Handles same-section, cross-section, adjacent-paragraph, and cross-category consolidation. Removes non-actionable noise. Use as part of the /review-document post-processing pipeline. ([JonnyCBB/claude-code-config](https://github.com/JonnyCBB/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/plugins/jiffy-toolkit/agents/doc-review-deduplicator.md))
- **doc-review-design-lens**: Information architecture, interaction states, user flows, missing design decisions. ([Pibomeister/agent-kit](https://github.com/Pibomeister/agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/doc-review-design-lens.md))
- **doc-review-feasibility**: Whether the approach survives contact with the codebase it will land in. ([Pibomeister/agent-kit](https://github.com/Pibomeister/agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/doc-review-feasibility.md))
- **doc-review-leader**: 문서 품질 점검 팀의 리더. 4명의 검증 에이전트를 조율하고, 결과를 종합하여 최종 수정 사항을 정리한다. ([asomi7007/eduelden-ai-deploy](https://github.com/asomi7007/eduelden-ai-deploy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-review-leader.md))
- **doc-review-product-lens**: Unsettled product positions and strategic weight. Domain-agnostic about who the users are. ([Pibomeister/agent-kit](https://github.com/Pibomeister/agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/doc-review-product-lens.md))
- **doc-review-scope-guardian**: Scope alignment and unjustified complexity; premature frameworks and abstractions. ([Pibomeister/agent-kit](https://github.com/Pibomeister/agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/doc-review-scope-guardian.md))
- **doc-review-security-lens**: Plan-level auth assumptions, data exposure, API surface and missing threat-model elements. Ordinary data handling is not a trigger. ([Pibomeister/agent-kit](https://github.com/Pibomeister/agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/doc-review-security-lens.md))
- **doc-reviewer**: Reviews Cosmos Gen3 documentation (docs/articles, README, CONTRIBUTING) for accuracy against the source and for the house writing style, which is connected prose paragraphs with a continuous thread rather than LLM-style bullet lists. Give it a list of .md files or a git range (e.g. gen3...HEAD); with nothing given it reviews the docs changed on the current branch. It reports by default and rewrites in place only when the caller asks it to fix. ([CosmosOS/Cosmos](https://github.com/CosmosOS/Cosmos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-reviewer.md) · ★3.2k)
- **doc-reviewer**: Use this agent to analyze and review documentation state, identifying gaps, outdated content, and quality issues. This agent only reviews and reports - it does not make changes. - **doc-reviewer**: Semantic reviewer for the in-repo documentation knowledge base. Use after a commit that touches docs (per-commit review — reports drift, read-only) or as an on-demand "doc-gardening" sweep over all of docs/ that opens a fix-up PR. It checks what the deterministic doc-linter cannot — whether each doc's CLAIMS still match the real code. Checks every point against its evidence, against primary sources (official docs, the org's repos, the library itself) and against every in-scope page, gives each point a verdict, writes the response in documentation/_responses/ and applies the accepted points to the pages in the review worktree. Launched by the /review-proposals skill, one per proposal; not for general use. ([MonashUniversity-ShockLab/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/MonashUniversity-ShockLab/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/shocklab-core/agents/doc-reviewer.md))
- **doc-reviewer**: Reviews documentation and comment quality in a git diff or directory. Flags missing JSDoc/TSDoc on public API, wrong or stale comments, commented-out code, redundant/noise comments, and spots where a missing comment is a real footgun. Use when asked to review docs/comments/JSDoc on a PR, diff, or subdirectory, or when the user says "check my comments", "review doc coverage", "are my JSDocs good", "review the docs on this PR". ([murphyjoseph/_playground](https://github.com/murphyjoseph/_playground/blob/HEAD/agents/doc-reviewer.md))
- **doc-reviewer**: 文档审查员 — 对需求文档进行三维审查（完整性/风险性/逻辑性），可 spawn 子智能体独立运行 ([NEGU30/pmcraft](https://github.com/NEGU30/pmcraft/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **doc-reviewer**: Reviews a PRD or doc for structure, clarity, and completeness before a PR. Read-only. ([nipja/pm-collab](https://github.com/nipja/pm-collab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-reviewer.md))
- **doc-reviewer**: Light, single-pass overview review of a feasibility, scope, or implementation-plan doc before execution. Returns PASS or REVISE with at most 5 findings. Spawns no subagents. Used by the ostack feature and optimize playbooks. ([omkargade04/ostack](https://github.com/omkargade04/ostack/blob/HEAD/agents/doc-reviewer.md))

([peachnireat-ship-it/secretary_test](https://github.com/peachnireat-ship-it/secretary_test/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-reviewer.md))
- **doc-reviewer**: Revisa la coherencia de la documentación. Obligatorio antes de cada merge a develop y en el cierre de cada fase. ([pirexia/plataforma-educativa](https://github.com/pirexia/plataforma-educativa/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-reviewer.md))
- **doc-reviewer**: doc-writer 에이전트가 작성한 기술 문서를 실제 프로젝트 구현 상황과 대조하는 문서 검수 담당. 파일을 고치지 않고 보고만 한다. doc-writer 작업이 끝나 사용자에게 문서 반영 결과를 보고하기 전에 호출한다. ([pistol357/study](https://github.com/pistol357/study/blob/HEAD/261028/.claude/agents/doc-reviewer.md))

- **doc-reviewer**: Reviews Spectio documentation with a fresh context — Progress pages, guides, PR bodies, changelog blocks, figures and media — against the rubric in docs/specs/documentation-system.md §4.2, re-runs sampled commands, opens every figure and screenshot, and returns approve / changes / block. - **doc-reviewer**: スライド（*.qmd）・設計書（*.md）などの資料を、書いたセッションとは別のコンテキストで検算レビューする。数値の出典、標本（分母）と層化の偏り、確定と未決の語調、ノートと本文の整合、本文と出典の射程を確かめ、正しさと資料としての適切さに影響する指摘だけを返す。案件に固有の観点と参照資料は、呼び出し時に渡されたファイルから読む。 ([spin-glass/tool-slides](https://github.com/spin-glass/tool-slides/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-reviewer.md))

- **doc-reviser**: Narrow-contract fixer. Applies the fixes a lore:doc-validator report names to exactly the targets it names — every mechanical and content finding of one round as one batch — and nothing else. It cannot fetch, cannot create files, cannot re-plan the document, and returns any finding it cannot apply exactly. Runs autonomously and cannot ask the user questions — pass it the validator report verbatim. ([hamidpl/lore](https://github.com/hamidpl/lore/blob/HEAD/plugins/lore/agents/doc-reviser.md) · ★3)
- **doc-reviser**: Produce una versión corregida (v2) de un documento markdown aplicando los hallazgos de su review report previo. Toma 3 inputs (doc original + review report + acceso al código) y produce un archivo nuevo `&lt;doc&gt;.v2.md` con TODOS los CRITICAL/HIGH/MEDIUM resueltos, manteniendo voz, estructura y secciones aprobadas. - **doc-rewriter**: tell-detector의 승인된 탐지 리포트를 받아 한국어 기술 문서를 교정하는 실행자. 의미/사실/수치/코드/register 불변 철칙 아래 승인 항목만 전수 교체한다. tech-doc-kr 스킬의 rules.md 대안 원칙(직역 관용구는 평서로 풀기, 개념어는 영어 유지, 별명은 코드 실명, 문답 프레임은 정리 절로)을 따른다. ([Jeon0976/tech-doc-kr](https://github.com/Jeon0976/tech-doc-kr/blob/HEAD/agents/doc-rewriter.md))
- **doc-rewriter**: Reescribe un documento markdown técnico en su versión definitiva y limpia — describiendo el estado actual del sistema con prosa clara y concisa, SIN citar paths, líneas o nombres de archivo, SIN bloques de "tras el audit", SIN metadocumentación de cambios. Es la capa final tras doc-reviser. Usar cuando un v2 corregido necesita pasar a documentación canónica user-facing. - **DOC-ROUTING**: Read `STATE.md` then root `AGENTS.md` first. Then open **one** of these if the task matches. Live code wins over all docs. ([UnboundAngel/RuForge](https://github.com/UnboundAngel/RuForge/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/DOC-ROUTING.md) · ★2)

"이 사진 스캔본으로 만들어줘", "문서 사진 펴줘", "스캔한 것처럼 해줘", "사진 여러 장 PDF 로 묶어줘" 요청 시 사용. 글자 인식(OCR)·요약은 하지 않는다. - **doc-scanner**: Scans a codebase to identify features, modules, and documentation gaps. Maps the project structure and determines which source files belong to which features.
